단어를 고를지, 문법은 맞는지 막막할 때가 많습니다. 영작에 익숙하지 않은 학생들에게
도움이 되는 것은 기본 문형입니다. 문형 몇 개만 자유롭게 활용할 수 있게 되면 다양한
문장에 확장해 적용할 수 있습니다.

 Americans at a Crossroads : Obama or McCain?

서론   Today Americans stand at a crossroads. Ahead lay two paths from which to choose. One leads to
four more years of current practices and policies while the other is a road in a new direction. This
coming Nov. 4 Americans will head to the ballot box to cast their vote for either a candidate that
represents change in every sense of the word or his challenger, a nominee who is a mere continua-
tion of the previous eight years of failed politics of the Bush administration. Considering the stark
realities, bleak future, and current hardships that Ametioans are now facing, there is only one real
choice. Obama is the right candidate to lead us out of the mess Bush got us into, and he is the man
that I will proudly support this November.                |

  A Candidate for Change
Obama is change. In fact, there has never been a candidate in American presidential history that
so thoroughly embodies change. From the color of his skin to the fact that he represents not only a
departure from the republican politics of recent years, but even a new, non-Clintonian face of the
Democratic Party.                                           |
Today, we live in an America that is embroiled in two ineffective wars and stands perilously close to

making ever since Bill Clinton lett office in 2000, with majority representation in all branches of the

legacy that they manage to leave behind? An economy in tatters, a military stretched thin as it
fights two unending wars, and a diplomatic program that has lowered the standing of the U.S.
across the world. If ever there were time for a change, it's now, and if ever there were a candidate

who embodies change, it is Barack Obama.

Diplomacy

In his eight-year tenure in office, President George W. Bush's confrontational and unyielding
stance on North Korea has pushed the hermit state not only to create a nuclear weapon but to
even carry out an underground test of the bomb. Obama has stated that he will reach out to
leaders of countries that the Bush administration has declared part of the “axis of evil” and offer
them direct negotiations with the U.S. Over the last eight years President Bush has refused

to negotiate directly with North Korea and Iran, and diplomatic relations with those two countries
have clearly suffered as a result. The failed diplomacy of the Bush administration has alienated
many foreign nations, and many have become distrusting of the U.S. and specifically, the
Republican Party. The only thing that can restore the trust of other nations in the U.S. is a new
face and a new party in power. Obama offers both of these changes, McCain offers neither.

Economy

If one simply reads the headlines of today's paper, it is easy to see what eight years of
Republican fiscal policies have brought us. Many of America’s greatest financial institutions simply
no longer exist. According to the New York Times, “47 U.S. banks have failed this year.” In addi-
tion, the U.S. stock market has lost 38.8 percent of its value so far this year. That's the largest
one-year value drop since The Great Depression in 1937. Unemployment, inflation, and mortgage
defaults are also at their highest levels in years. In his first year in office, Obama plans to offer a
$1,000 tax cut for the middle class families that are currently being hit hardest. He will also pro-
vide a $50 billion package aimed at jumpstarting the economy and preventing one million
Americans from losing their jobs. It is clear that Obama has his mind set on returning stability to
the tumultuous U.S. economy.

On issues most important to Americans these days - diplomacy, the ongoing wars, and the econo-
my - Obama will bring innovation and creativity to the White House. He has shown us his promis-
ing vision for repairing the U.S. and politicians and experts agree that it is the best course for
the nation. The last thing the American people need now is four more years of disastrous policies
in Washington. We call for someone who will take America in a completely new direction, and that

man is Barack Obama.

Imagine that on a feverishly hot summer's day in Korea, a man is sitting on an

dog’s meat. He will finish his meal and gain the energy he needs to finish his full day's

Suppose this man were to come across another common individual but with western

First, the junk foods are unhealthy for you because they contain bad
nutrients. Some of these bad nutrients are fat and sugar. The sugar slows
down your growth and the fat makes you gain weight and lazy. For
example, there is one kid in my school who weighs more than the other
kids and I asked, “Hey, what to you eat the most with your family?” He

When I heard the answer, I was pretty shocked about the big affect that

increase the blood pressure. Therefore, junk foods are really unhealthy
for your body.

In addition, junk foods are unhealthy for your body because they
make you slow. If people depend too much on fast food, they get too

on streets, impolite manners, and adverse sentiments against foreigners.
However, there are several positive aspects of Korea that can change the

easy for people to learn and use. Thus, the percentage of illiterates has
dramatically decreased since Hangul was made about 500 years ago.

who are famous around the world. Yong-joon Bae, for instance, is very

in China, Japan, and other Asian countries. Therefore, lots of foreigners
visit Korea to find out more about HanRyu-Star’s homeland. B-boys are

now offers many opportunities for foreigners as individuals as well as
companies. For example, many leading companies use Korea as the test

===== PAGE 98 =====
Our Territory Forever, Dokdo.

첨삭 후 수정된 완성 에세이입니다. AS 문장에서 어떤 부분이 달라졌는지 비교해 보세요.

Recently, Japan announced a plan to include its sovereignty claim
over Dokdo in its middle school curriculum. To cope with the problem,
the Korean government has taken action. But what is it that we can do
to address the Dokdo problem? There are two easy and important things
which we can do: we can study about Dokdo and tell many foreigners
about the issue.

First, we should study about Dokdo. In other words we need to have
more interest in Dokdo. There are many ways to study about Dokdo.
We can use newspapers, books or the Internet. Through such methods,
we can study more deeply about Dokdo. According to a program that
I saw, Japanese people research a lot about Dokdo. They study about
Dokdo very hard and try to find information. Like this, we should try to
research about Dokdo in Korea. We should try to know more about our
territory Dokdo through all available media. By doing this, we can save
Dokdo from Japan.

The second thing that we can do is to tell others in foreign nations
about Dokdo. Recently. a famous Korean singer Kim Jang-Hoon used
his money to take out an ad about Dokdo in the U. S. newspaper, The
New York Times. We can’t do these things because we are so young, but
we can tell others in other ways. For example, those who have a foreign
friend as a pen pal can tell them the truth about Dokdo. Also these days
most children go to English academies. In that case, they can tell their
English teachers about Dokdo. When we tell one person about Dokdo,
that person will probably tell another person and the truth would
spread. Eventually, everybody in the world will know the truth.



===== PAGE 99 =====
| 결론             To conclude, we should study about Dokdo and tell many people
about Dokdo to resolve the Dokdo problem. We can’t negotiate with

Japan about Dokdo because we are young. So we just have to do our best
to protect Dokdo. Then, we can grow up to be proud Koreans.

habitually post maliciously. They will “file
for arrest warrants and ’ take other action
against those who are active in Internet
bullying and defamation.

Police will also request portal site
operators to strengthen monitoring on
cyber bullying and remove malicious
postings.

In the meantime, police are investigating
to find who first spread rumors about Choi
that “linked her to the death of another

actor Ahn Jae-hwan. The investigation
"16 expected to expand to the brokerage
industry as analysts are collecting gossip
about politicians, entertainers and top
corporate executives and spreading it to
their clients. "Most of this information
usually spreads to the Internet eventually.

Choi’s death "prompted the ruling
Grand National Party to push for the
“Cyber Slander Law” to punish those who
insult others with their malicious online
comments. It plans to '’seek parliamentary
approval for the bill at a session this fall.

Opposition parties are denouncing the
move as it could undermine the freedom of
speech. “The main opposition Democratic
Party is claiming that ‘the ruling party is
attempting to remove anti-government
postings.

For example, when I was a fourth grader in elementary school, I made
an internet cafe with some friends, which was popular among us. We
wrote stories, shared opinions, chatted and uploaded our pictures. The
most fun thing was seeing each other's pictures and talking about them.
After making our internet cafe public, there were a lot of malicious
replies. Therefore some of my friends were injured from those bad
words. So, we changed our policy, so that only members of the cafe
could write comments. We found that there were considerably less
malicious comments after we changed policy. I think a Cyber Slander
Law wiil be similar in principle, the experiences of my past events. Thus,
a Cyber Slander Law can be effective to reduce malicious online replies
and any damage resulting from them.

A Cyber Slander Law will be a very positive influence on society. The
reason is that malicious online comments will decline because people’s
names will be public, so they will write articles or comments more
carefully. Thus, over time, malicious replies will diminish. Then victims
of such replies will decrease, too. I believe that we have to establish cyber
order as soon as possible, and a Cyber Slander Law will help it happen.

In the present, people waste so many resources without thinking
about how materials are. Since fuel is running out, it causes a severe
inflation in all costs. Accordingly, it is difficult to find the way to solve.
At first, we have to consider two things-economy and energy.

First of all, the government has to change its vision—higher economic
growth-to secure the economy. During the inauguration of the
president this year, he promised us a high rate of growth in the economy.
However, present situation shows that the projection of growing is not
that good. It means the government need to hold the line of material

prices and make economic basis stronger.

The innovation of replacement energy technology is also an
important mission, because the main reason for the rise of inflation is
due to oil prices. A sudden rise in oil prices is because there is a scare that
oil is running out. Therefore, we need to get enough energy from other
resources, before oil completely runs out. If the government finds new
technology to make energy, methane gas from trash or fierce winds can

be significant resources.

Nations must also be more careful. At first, people have to take care
of their outgo. Because of the high rise in oil prices, regular families
will be hit the hardest. The best way to minimize damages is to shrink

expenditure and save more money for emergencies or lower expenses.

And also, saving is another way for dealing with an economical
depression. Since this is caused lack of fossil fuels, minimizing its use

may be the best method. You don’t need to stop using all things that use

Rate raised market participants’ concerns

The United States, as a superpower, has always been a country that has influenced
other nations. Korea, as well, cannot be exempt from this. However, due to the
recent decline in the value of the Korean won to the American dollar, the Korean
economy may suffer more critically than society may have predicted. Already, the

prices of homes and the cost of living is still higher than before in much of Korean

society—and with the stock market experiencing such turmoil, it is undeniable that

Korea will have to feel its effects as well, not only as a large conglomerate, but as

individuals as well.

Appreciating the Won

첨삭 후 수정된 완성 에세이입니다. 처음 문장에서 어떤 부분이 달라졌는지 비교해 보세요.

The value of the won is depreciating sharply, which is shaking the
entire economy and the stock market. Correspondingly, the exchange
rate is soaring without any obstacles and again, the dollar holds strongly
against the won, it is at its highest since May 16, 2002. Moreover, as the
economy of Wall Street and the USA fall, Korea-which depends on the
US heavily-cannot avoid its bad influences and aftereffects. Because the
size of The Korean economy is small and fragile, even a tremor in this

sort of situation can affect it critically.

As the exchange rate fluctuates, natural resources and oil prices are
increasing and consumer price rises, too. In the case of Korea, which
imports all of its resources, this rise can be very dangerous because it
discourages imports and exhausts the economy. As foreign investors
retrieve their investments, Korea’s foreign exchange reserves are being

vacant fast. And this is one of the factors that helps the exchange rate.

Koreans have to save, recycle and reuse natural resources, whose
prices are soaring dramatically. Also, they should be able to control
themselves not to purchase overseas products or travel foreign countries

during this distressful time just for themselves.

So, my solution is to appreciate the original value of the won. To
achieve that goal, we have to draw attention from foreign investors
again, because it is very necessary to enrich the exchange reserves.
Also the government must support imports. Its previous goal to boost

exports just went down the drain, leaving an increased exchange rate.



===== PAGE 139 =====
Thus, the government’s plan to boost exports came to nothing and
they have to change their attitude. The side effect-the skyrocketing
exchange rate-must be rectified. This will reveal the ability and skill of
crisis management in a new government, president and other national
officials belonging to that fields. Therefore, they must do their best to
overcome this international crisis.

percent in France, 48 percent in the U.K.,

무학중학교 1학년 김현수

Many people have different opinions about how teachers should
teach their students. Some people say teachers should hit students when
they do not listen or when they cannot answer the questions. Others
claim teachers should only teach students who can understand them
and should leave behind other students who cannot take in the lessons.

Main topic

Such opinions cannot be neglected. However the best ways to make

the students actively participate in class are to teach them with more

materials and to make the students question more freely.

Topic sentence

Teaching the students with more informations will help students

understand the subjects more easily. In Korea, only 35.2 percent of

the students said the class was interesting. More than 80 percent of the
students stated that they could not fully understand the lessons. By

Supporting sentence

having more pictures or descriptions, students would be interested in

the class even though it would take more time to prepare. For example,
students learned about rocks in science. If teachers only explained the
stones in words, some students would have hard time grasping the
lectures. However, if teachers brought the stones or showed pictures
of the stones to the class, students would take the lessons with more
interest. They would make out the differences between each rocks
without difficulty. More entertaining activities, therefore, are one of the
finest methods to teach.

Topic sentence

Letting students discuss freely in front of the class would help

students in studying. Only 18.3 percent of the Korean students

said they liked studying. More than half of the students were bored.
Teachers’ severe ways of punishment have put dictatorship in classes.
Students cannot ask questions because they fear teachers would

Supporting sentence

discipline the students’ ignorance. By making an environment where

students can question and debate openly, students would be eager



===== PAGE 147 =====
Al

to learn. For instance, if teachers made the students to listen to their

class without remarks, students would not know the answers to their
questions. They would certainly feel the class uninteresting. But, if the
teachers accepted the students’ inquiries with pleasure, the students
would know more about the sessions and their grades would soar up.
Discussing freely, then, is the other supreme technique to educate.

Students have many opinions about how their teachers should
teach the students. Some students insist teachers should always be
kind to others. Few students say teachers should not hit students very

Concluding remarks

hard. Their ideas cannot be disregarded. Nevertheless, teaching the

students with more informations and teaching in the circumstance

where students can find answer to their problems are the superior

ways to develop students. It would take much time to prepare for the
pictures or representations. Teachers would find it hard to make up
for the students. Also, teachers would have a tough time answering all
the student's questions. Still, these two ways can both give good effects
on both students and teachers. Students would study more hard with
interest and teachers would also learn about things they never knew
by getting ready for the researches. By the two solutions, classes would
fill with students’ laughter, discussing about projects they learned with

amusement.

The Best Way to Teach Students

첨삭 후 수정된 완성 에세이입니다. 처음 문장에서 어떤 부분이 달라졌는지 비교해 보세요.

Many people have different opinions about how teachers should
teach their students. Some people say teachers should hit students
when they do not listen or if they cannot answer the questions. Others
claim teachers should only teach students who can understand them
and should leave behind other students who cannot keep up with the
lessons. Such opinions cannot be ignored. However the best ways to
make students actively participate in class are to teach them with more

materials and to make students question more freely.

Teaching students with more information will help students
understand the subjects more easily. In Korea, only 35.2 percent of the
students said class was interesting. More than 80 percent of the students
stated that they could not fully understand the lessons. By employing
more pictures or descriptions, students would be interested in the
class even though it would take more time to prepare. For example, if
students were learning about rocks in science, and teachers only explain
the stones in words, some students would have hard time grasping the
lectures. However, if teachers brought the stones or showed pictures
of the stones to the class, students would take the lessons with more
interest. They would be able to make out the differences between each
rocks without difficulty. More entertaining activities, therefore, are one
of the finest methods to teach.

Letting students discuss freely in front of the class would help
students. Only 18.3 percent of Korean students said they liked
studying. More than half of the students were bored. Teachers’ severe
ways of punishment have brought a dictatorship in classes. Students
cannot ask questions because they fear teachers would discipline them

for their ignorance. By making an environment where students can

|
Pais               1
St gs ih owe ene



===== PAGE 155 =====
question and debate openly, students would be eager to learn. For
instance, if teachers made the students listen to their class without
remarks, students would not know the answers to their questions. They
would certainly feel the class uninteresting. But, if the teachers accepted
the students’ inquiries with pleasure, the students would know more
about the sessions and their grades would soar. Discussing freely, then, is
the other supreme technique to educate.

Students have many opinions about how their teachers should teach
the students. Some students insist teachers should always be kind. Few
students say teachers should not hit students very hard. Their ideas
cannot be disregarded. Nevertheless, teaching the students with more
information and teaching in the circumstance where students can find
answer to their problems are the superior ways to develop students. It
would take much time to prepare pictures or representations. Teachers
would find it hard to provide make-up lectures for the students. Also,
teachers would have a tough time answering all the student's questions.
Still, these two ways can have a good effects on both students and
teachers. Students would study more hard with interest and teachers
would also learn about things they never knew by getting ready for the
research. By the two solutions, classes would fill with students’ laughter,
discussing projects they learned with amusement.

5 안성에세이

With Easy English Comes an

Easy World

첨삭 후 수정된 완성 에세이입니다. 처음 문장에서 어떤 부분이 달라졌는지 비교해 보세요.

As the world is globalizing, people need to communicate with
foreigners frequently for business, negotiation and so on. This is the
reason why English became necessary for people to learn around the
world-and it is no exception for Koreans. Thus, Koreans spend a lot of
time and money learning English. However, according to the British
Council, Korea ranked 19th on the general training module of the
IELTS among 20 countires. Therefore, it is obvious that we must
modify the way in which English is taught in the Korean education
system.

First of all, we should do away with the cramming of English
vocabulary in the Korean education system. Almost all Korean high
school students are taught to memorize every single word in their
textbooks for exams. However, this does not benefit students because,
most of the vocabulary that people learn during English classes are
quite different from the way in which native speakers write and speak.
Because of this, the efficiency to learn English declines and people who
do not like English may not be able to have a chance to become familiar
with English.

Secondly, we need to focus not only on grammar and reading
comprehension but also on writing and speaking, which is more
practical for the application of English. For example, watching movies
in English is a good way to learn practical English easily. People can
learn a lot of words and idioms by watching movies in English. Debate
class is another way to improve English without burdens. Then people
will be able to enhance both the ability to speak English and the ability

가 6             ㆍ
to express one’s ideas logically.



===== PAGE 167 =====
             Simply cramming Vocabulary and Grammar into one’s head is not
the only way to improve. Koreans should realize these problems in the
learning of English in the Korean education system and think deeply
about what will really help improve people use English with ease.

I agree with grouping students into different classes for English and
math, but I disagree with that policy when it is applied to all subjects.

I think grouping students into different levels for English and math
would be a more efficient way of studying. It makes it easier for teachers
to teach and for students to learn. Some students are so smart or well
prepared that they can understand well but some students cannot.
The teachers in leveled classes can find the best way to make students
understand better. The students will be able to get more enjoyment out
of class.

On the other hand, leveled classes for all subjects can put too
much stress on the students. A school is not just for studying. We also
should learn sociality and propriety at school to live our lives properly
as members of society. I believe it is the difference from the private

competitiveness, we cannot afford to learn the other important things
except simply studying subjects.

===== PAGE 189 =====
완성에세이

서론

Two Ways to Fight Global
Warming from Home

첨삭 후 수정된 완성 에세이입니다. AS 문장에서 어떤 부분이 달라졌는지 비교해 보세요.

As time passes, global warming is growing in severity. Many
environmental organizations try to play up the seriousness of the issue.
But what are some things that we can do from home to prevent this
crisis? I have two suggestions.

First, we must lessen the amount of trash, because it creates a
tremendous quantity of carbon dioxide. For example, we should reduce
our use of disposable products. Most of them are not biodegradable and
accumulate for thousands of years, which causes serious soil problems
that lead to the creation of massive amounts of CO). But even aside
from the increases in pollution, it is important that we reuse such
products.

In addition, we must practice moderation in our daily lives.
For example, we should use mass transportation for long-distance
travel, and bicycles for everything close by. In summer, from both
an environmental and a health standpoint, it is better to leave the
air conditioner off. When not properly cleaned and maintained, air
conditioners fill with dust and can actually be bad for your health.

However, the most important thing is that we keep these thing in
mind and bring them into play. If our knowledge of the environment is
not put into use, what good is thinking about it? The simple suggestions
above are not difficult, so we can easily adhere to them. I want everyone
to enjoy life in the future. For a better life tomorrow, I hope everyone to
take my suggestions to heart.

될 수 있다. 하지만 그와 같은 ASE 많지 않다. 그래서 나는 정부가 도와야 한다고 생각했다. 정부
는 이와 같은 일에 기여해야 하고 자발적으로 발 벗고 나서야 한다. 정부는 가장 잘 도울 수 있는 유일
한 누군가일 수 있다.

이후에도 우리는 피해를 복구해야 한다. 여전히 다른 사고가 발생할 수 있다. 그것은 여전히 가능
하다. 모든 사람들은 지금부터 주의해야 한다. 지금부터 이런 사고를 만들지 말아야 한다. 그러면 사
고를 만들지 않기 위해 취할 수 있는 어떤 방법들이 있을까? 첫째, 환경오염을 멈추기 위해 새로운 에
너지를 개발해야 한다. 단지 새 에너지를 개발하는 것 뿐 아니라 우리는 에너지를 많이 사용하지 말아
야 한다. 에너지 사용은 환경오염을 일으킬 것이다. 에너지를 많이 사용하는 것은 다른 나라로부터 에
너지 자원을 수입하는 것을 의미한다. 그러면 또 다른 원유 유출이 발생할 수도 있다. 우리는 에너지

was about the animal cloning, and the program discussed Snuppy. Life
science is my favorite science area, so I watched it with interest. After
the program was over, I thought it was so amazing that animal cloning
can help humans. In my opinion, animal cloning must be continued.
It helps humans to solve the causes of diseases and promotes the rate of

propagates of endangered species.

First of all, animal cloning helps humans to find the cures of diseases
such as leukemia, diabetes, and parkinsonism. For almost 30 years,
scientists have been trying to find the cures of these diseases to make
medicines by testing animal. Therefore by animal cloning, scientists

could experiment better and could develop medicines.

Also, animal cloning can help propagate endangered species. These
days, there are so many species which are endangered by hunters. We
should protect these species from dangers. If we use somatic cell cloning
technology, it is possible to propagate endangered species. Sometimes,
it is difficult to make species do natural mating. This technology might

help various species to multiply more effectively.

Finally, high species can be propagated as well. For example, it is
hard to have many good breeds. But, we can use somatic cell cloning
to propagate good breeds as well as endangered species. It will help our
environments to be preserved.

multi-stage booster rockets and the Soyuz
